#Rifaat
country_event = {
	id = SYR_Rifaat_turkish.1
	
	title = SYR_Rifaat_turkish.1.t
	desc = SYR_Rifaat_turkish.1.d
	picture = GFX_handshake_black

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		tag = TUR
		from = {
			tag = SYR
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = SYR_Rifaat_turkish.1.a
		set_temp_variable = { receiver_nation = TUR.id }
		set_temp_variable = { sender_nation = SYR.id }
		set_improved_trade_agreement = yes
		set_temp_variable = { target_nation = SYR.id }
		set_temp_variable = { adding_nation = TUR.id }
		change_permanent_investment_target = yes
		set_temp_variable = { target_nation = TUR.id }
		set_temp_variable = { adding_nation = SYR.id }
		change_permanent_investment_target = yes
		one_random_industrial_complex = yes
		SYR = {
			country_event = {
				id = SYR_Rifaat_turkish.2
				days = 1
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 50

			modifier = {
				factor = 50

				has_opinion = {
					target = SYR
					value > 10
				}
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = SYR_Rifaat_turkish.1.b

		SYR = {
			country_event = {
				id = SYR_Rifaat_turkish.3
				days = 1
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 50
			modifier = {
				factor = -50
				has_opinion = {
					target = SYR
					value < 0
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

country_event = {
	id = SYR_Rifaat_british.1
	
	title = SYR_Rifaat_british.1.t
	desc = SYR_Rifaat_british.1.d
	picture = GFX_handshake_black

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		tag = ENG
		from = {
			tag = SYR
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = SYR_Rifaat_british.1.a

		ai_chance = {
			base = 50

			modifier = {
				factor = 50

				has_opinion = {
					target = SYR
					value > 10
				}
			}
		}

		set_temp_variable = { receiver_nation = ENG.id }
		set_temp_variable = { sender_nation = SYR.id }
		set_improved_trade_agreement = yes

		set_temp_variable = { target_nation = SYR.id }
		set_temp_variable = { adding_nation = ENG.id }
		change_permanent_investment_target = yes
		
		set_temp_variable = { target_nation = ENG.id }
		set_temp_variable = { adding_nation = SYR.id }
		change_permanent_investment_target = yes
		
		add_timed_idea = {
			idea = SYR_british_partnership
			days = 800
		}

		SYR = {
			country_event = {
				id = SYR_Rifaat_british.2
				days = 1
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = SYR_Rifaat_british.1.b

		ai_chance = {
			base = 50

			modifier = {
				factor = -50

				has_opinion = {
					target = SYR
					value < 0
				}
			}
		}

		SYR = {
			country_event = {
				id = SYR_Rifaat_british.3
				days = 1
			}
		}
	}
}

country_event = {

	id = Syrianew.1

	title = Syrianew.1.t
	desc = Syrianew.1.d
	picture = GFX_SYR_tahrir_al_sham

	is_triggered_only = yes

	fire_only_once = yes

	option = {
		name = Syrianew.1.a
		log = "[GetDateText]: [This.GetName]: Syrianew.1.a executed"
		add_stability = 0.02
		add_political_power = 100
		set_temp_variable = { add_col_one = 7 }
		add_coalition_members_effect = yes
		subtract_from_variable = {
			party_pop_array^7 = current_cons_popularity
		}
		recalculate_party = yes
		update_government_coalition_strength = yes
		ai_chance = {
			base = 10
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = Syrianew.1.b
		log = "[GetDateText]: [This.GetName]: Syrianew.1.b executed"
		add_war_support = 0.05
		add_popularity = { ideology = nationalist popularity = 0.15 }
		set_temp_variable = { add_col_one = 21 }
		add_coalition_members_effect = yes
		subtract_from_variable = {
			party_pop_array^21 = current_cons_popularity
		}
		recalculate_party = yes
		update_government_coalition_strength = yes
		ai_chance = {
			base = 10
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = Syrianew.1.c
		log = "[GetDateText]: [This.GetName]: Syrianew.1.c executed"
		add_war_support = 0.02
		add_political_power = 50
		ai_chance = {
			base = 10
		}
	}
}

#Fate of Rojava
country_event = {
	id = SyriaFocus.0
	title = SyriaFocus.0.t
	desc = SyriaFocus.0.desc
	picture = GFX_SYR_rojava
	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = SyriaFocus.0.a
		log = "[GetDateText]: [This.GetName]: SyriaFocus.0.a executed"		#The area belongs to Syria
		ai_chance = {
			base = 75
			modifier = {
				factor = 0.8
				tag = FSA
			}
		}
		ROJ = { country_event = { days = 5 id = SyriaFocus.1 } }
		set_country_flag = Rojava_Question_Solved
	}
	option = {
		name = SyriaFocus.0.b
		log = "[GetDateText]: [This.GetName]: SyriaFocus.0.b executed"		#Offer them federal status
		ai_chance = {
			base = 20
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				tag = NUS
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 1.5
				tag = FSA
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				original_tag = SYR
			}
		}
		ROJ = { country_event = { days = 5 id = SyriaFocus.4 } }
		set_country_flag = Rojava_Question_Solved
	}
	option = {
		name = SyriaFocus.0.c
		log = "[GetDateText]: [This.GetName]: SyriaFocus.0.c executed"		#Let them be independent
		ai_chance = {
			base = 5
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				tag = NUS
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 2
				tag = FSA
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				original_tag = SYR
			}
		}
		add_political_power = -100
		remove_state_core = 193
		remove_state_core = 571
		remove_state_core = 575
		set_country_flag = Rojava_Question_Solved
		hidden_effect = { news_event = { id = SyriaFocusNews.0 hours = 6 } }
	}
}

#Try to subjugate Rojava
country_event = {
	id = SyriaFocus.1
	title = SyriaFocus.1.t
	desc = SyriaFocus.1.desc
	picture = GFX_SYR_rojava
	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = SyriaFocus.1.a
		log = "[GetDateText]: [This.GetName]: SyriaFocus.1.a executed"		#Reject demands
		ai_chance = {
			base = 80
			modifier = {
				factor = 0.875
				FROM = { tag = FSA }
			}
		}
		FROM = { country_event = { days = 5 id = SyriaFocus.2 } }
	}
	option = {
		name = SyriaFocus.1.b
		log = "[GetDateText]: [This.GetName]: SyriaFocus.1.b executed"		#Agree
		ai_chance = {
			base = 20
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				FROM = { tag = NUS }
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 1.5
				FROM = { tag = FSA }
			}
		}
		custom_effect_tooltip = GAME_OVER_TT
		FROM = { country_event = { days = 5 id = SyriaFocus.3 } }
	}
}

#Demands refused
country_event = {
	id = SyriaFocus.2
	title = SyriaFocus.2.t
	desc = SyriaFocus.2.desc
	picture = GFX_SYR_rojava
	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = SyriaFocus.2.a
		log = "[GetDateText]: [This.GetName]: SyriaFocus.2.a executed"		#Damn them
		ai_chance = {
			base = 90
		}
		create_wargoal = { type = annex_everything target = ROJ }
		remove_state_core = 193
		remove_state_core = 571
		remove_state_core = 575
		add_state_claim = 193
		add_state_claim = 571
		add_state_claim = 575
		hidden_effect = { news_event = { id = SyriaFocusNews.3 hours = 6 } }
		set_country_flag = SYR_Rojava_go_to_war
		add_ai_strategy = {
			type = conquer
			id = "ROJ"
			value = 200
		}
		add_ai_strategy = {
			type = antagonize
			id = "ROJ"
			value = 200
		}
		add_ai_strategy = {
			type = declare_war
			id = "ROJ"
			value = 200
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_non_aggression_pact_with = ROJ }
			diplomatic_relation = {
				country = ROJ
				relation = non_aggression_pact
				active = no
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = SyriaFocus.2.b
		log = "[GetDateText]: [This.GetName]: SyriaFocus.2.b executed"		#Let them go
		ai_chance = {
			base = 10
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				original_tag = SYR
			}
		}
		add_political_power = -100
		remove_state_core = 193
		remove_state_core = 571
		remove_state_core = 575
		add_state_claim = 193
		add_state_claim = 571
		add_state_claim = 575
		hidden_effect = { news_event = { id = SyriaFocusNews.0 hours = 6 } }
	}
}

#Federal Status accepted
country_event = {
	id = SyriaFocus.5
	title = SyriaFocus.5.t
	desc = SyriaFocus.5.desc
	picture = GFX_SYR_rojava
	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = SyriaFocus.5.a
		log = "[GetDateText]: [This.GetName]: SyriaFocus.5.a executed"		#Huzzah
		ai_chance = { base = 100 }
		every_owned_state = {
			limit = {
				is_core_of = ROJ
			}
			ROJ = { transfer_state = PREV }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_global_flag = GAME_RULE_allow_colored_puppets
			}
			set_autonomy = {
				target = ROJ
				autonomy_state = autonomy_autonomous_state_colored
				end_wars = yes
				end_civil_wars = yes
			}
		}
		else = {
			set_autonomy = {
				target = ROJ
				autonomy_state = autonomy_autonomous_state
				end_wars = yes
				end_civil_wars = yes
			}
		}
		hidden_effect = { news_event = { id = SyriaFocusNews.2 hours = 6 } }
	}
}

#Russian and Iranian opinion on SSNP
country_event = {
	id = SyriaFocus.6
	title = SyriaFocus.6.t
	desc = SyriaFocus.6.desc
	picture = GFX_SYR_ssnp
	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = SyriaFocus.6.a
		log = "[GetDateText]: [This.GetName]: SyriaFocus.6.a executed"		#Keep SSNP around
		ai_chance = {
			base = 50
			modifier = {
				factor = 1.5
				tag = SOV
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0.5
				tag = PER
			}
		}
		set_country_flag = Opinion_On_SSNP_Keep
		SYR = { country_event = SyriaFocus.7 }
	}
	option = {
		name = SyriaFocus.6.b
		log = "[GetDateText]: [This.GetName]: SyriaFocus.6.b executed"		#Purge SSNP
		ai_chance = {
			base = 50
			modifier = {
				factor = 0.5
				tag = SOV
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 1.5
				tag = PER
			}
		}
		set_country_flag = Opinion_On_SSNP_Purge
		SYR = { country_event = SyriaFocus.7 }
	}
}

#SSNP Coup
country_event = {
	id = SyriaFocus.8
	hidden = yes
	is_triggered_only = yes
	fire_only_once = yes

	immediate = {
		random_list = {
			10 = { country_event = SyriaFocus.9 }		#Assad dies, strong civil war
			70 = { country_event = SyriaFocus.10 }		#Medium civil war
			20 = { country_event = SyriaFocus.11 }		#Low scale civil war
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = SyriaFocus.8.a
		log = "[GetDateText]: [This.GetName]: SyriaFocus.8.a executed"
		ai_chance = { base = 100 }
	}
}

country_event = {
	id = SyriaFocus.9
	title = SyriaFocus.9.t
	desc = SyriaFocus.9.d
	picture = GFX_Assad_parade

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = SyriaFocus.9.a
		log = "[GetDateText]: [This.GetName]: SyriaFocus.9.a executed"
		ai_chance = { base = 100 }
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_leader = { name = "Bashar Al Assad" ruling_only = yes } }
			kill_country_leader = yes
			set_global_flag = Al_Assad_Dead
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				nationalist > 40
				NOT = { has_country_flag = Second_Syrian_Civil_War }
			}
			start_civil_war = {
				ideology = nationalist
				size = 0.80
			}
			set_country_flag = Second_Syrian_Civil_War
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				nationalist > 30
				NOT = { has_country_flag = Second_Syrian_Civil_War }
			}
			start_civil_war = {
				ideology = nationalist
				size = 0.60
			}
			set_country_flag = Second_Syrian_Civil_War
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				nationalist > 20
				NOT = { has_country_flag = Second_Syrian_Civil_War }
			}
			start_civil_war = {
				ideology = nationalist
				size = 0.40
			}
			set_country_flag = Second_Syrian_Civil_War
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				nationalist > 10
				NOT = { has_country_flag = Second_Syrian_Civil_War }
			}
			start_civil_war = {
				ideology = nationalist
				size = 0.20
			}
			set_country_flag = Second_Syrian_Civil_War
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { has_country_flag = Second_Syrian_Civil_War }
			}
			start_civil_war = {
				ideology = nationalist
				size = 0.10
			}
			set_country_flag = Second_Syrian_Civil_War
		}
	}
}

				nationalist > 30

			}

			}
			set_country_flag = Second_Syrian_Civil_War
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { has_country_flag = Second_Syrian_Civil_War }
			}
			start_civil_war = {
				ideology = nationalist
				size = 0.10
			}
			set_country_flag = Second_Syrian_Civil_War
		}
	}

		}

			add_opinion_modifier = { target = FSA modifier = barbarian_practices }
		}

				}

		}

			type = annex_everything
		}

		hidden_effect = { news_event = { id = SyriaFocusNews.6 hours = 6 } }
	}

}

	}

				release = DRU

			}
			if = {
				limit = { DRU = { is_subject_of = ROOT } }
				set_autonomy = {
					target = DRU
					autonomy_state = autonomy_free
				}
			}
			#Set a random ideology
			DRU = {
				random_list = {
					10 = {
						set_politics = { ruling_party = democratic elections_allowed = yes }
						add_popularity = { ideology = democratic popularity = 0.6 }
					}
					20 = {
						set_politics = { ruling_party = communism elections_allowed = yes }
						add_popularity = { ideology = communism popularity = 0.6 }
					}
					40 = {
						set_politics = { ruling_party = neutrality elections_allowed = yes }
						add_popularity = { ideology = neutrality popularity = 0.6 }
					}
					10 = {
						set_politics = { ruling_party = nationalist elections_allowed = no }
						add_popularity = { ideology = nationalist popularity = 0.6 }
					}
				}
			}
		}
		declare_war_on = {
			target = DRU
			type = annex_everything
		}

#Mejd Invited
country_event = {
	id = SyriaFocus.43
	title = SyriaFocus.43.t
	desc = SyriaFocus.43.d
	picture = GFX_syria_medj_madness

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = SyriaFocus.43.a
		log = "[GetDateText]: [This.GetName]: SyriaFocus.43.a executed"
		ai_chance = { base = 30 }
		hidden_effect = {
			set_temp_variable = { party_index = 23 }
			set_temp_variable = { party_popularity_increase = party_pop_array^23 }
			add_relative_party_popularity = yes
			clear_array = ruling_party
			clear_array = gov_coalition_array
			add_to_array = { ruling_party = 23 }
			add_to_array = { gov_coalition_array = 7 }
			add_to_array = { gov_coalition_array = 15 }
			update_government_coalition_strength = yes
			update_party_name = yes
			set_coalition_drift = yes
			set_ruling_leader = yes
			set_leader = yes
			set_politics = {
				ruling_party = nationalist
				elections_allowed = no
			}
			create_country_leader = {
				name = "Mejd al-Assad"
				picture = "mejd_al_assad.dds"
				ideology = Monarchist
				traits = {
					nationalist_Monarchist
					inexperienced_imperialist
					incapable
					king
					warmonger
				}
			}
		}
		recalculate_party = yes
	}
}
#Rifaat Invited Back
country_event = {
	id = SyriaFocus.44
	title = SyriaFocus.44.t
	desc = SyriaFocus.44.d
	picture = GFX_rifaat_al_assad

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = SyriaFocus.44.a
		log = "[GetDateText]: [This.GetName]: SyriaFocus.44.a executed"
		ai_chance = { base = 30 }
		hidden_effect = {
			set_temp_variable = { party_index = 0 }
			set_temp_variable = { party_popularity_increase = party_pop_array^0 }
			add_relative_party_popularity = yes
			clear_array = ruling_party
			clear_array = gov_coalition_array
			add_to_array = { ruling_party = 0 }
			add_to_array = { gov_coalition_array = 7 }
			add_to_array = { gov_coalition_array = 15 }
			add_to_array = { gov_coalition_array = 23 }
			update_government_coalition_strength = yes
			update_party_name = yes
			set_coalition_drift = yes
			set_ruling_leader = yes
			set_leader = yes
			set_politics = {
				ruling_party = democratic
				elections_allowed = no
			}
			create_country_leader = {
				name = "Rifaat al-Assad"
				picture = "Rifaat_Al-Assad.dds"
				ideology = Western_Autocracy
				traits = {
					western_Western_Autocracy
					political_prisoner
					democratic_reformer
					staunch_personalist
					cornered_fox
					corruptible
				}
			}
		}
		recalculate_party = yes
	}
}
#Statement of 99
country_event = {
	id = SyriaFocus.45
	title = SyriaFocus.45.t
	desc = SyriaFocus.45.d
	picture = GFX_damascus_spring

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		hidden_effect = { country_event = { id = SyriaFocus.46 days = 90 random = 1440 } }
	}

	option = {
		name = SyriaFocus.45.a		#
		log = "[GetDateText]: [This.GetName]: SyriaFocus.45.a executed"
		ai_chance = {
			base = 70
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				has_global_flag = SYR_NEW_ASSAD_FOCUS_PATH
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { party_index = 12 }
		set_temp_variable = { party_popularity_increase = 0.10 }
		set_temp_variable = { temp_outlook_increase = 0.10 }
		add_relative_party_popularity = yes
	}
	option = {
		name = SyriaFocus.45.b		#
		log = "[GetDateText]: [This.GetName]: SyriaFocus.45.b executed"
		ai_chance = {
			base = 30
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				OR = {
					has_global_flag = SYR_HISTORICAL_FOCUS_PATH
					has_global_flag = SYR_IRON_LADY_FOCUS_PATH
					has_global_flag = SYR_SYRIAN_SAVAGE_FOCUS_PATH
				}
			}
		}
		add_political_power = -150
	}
}

#Statement of 1000
country_event = {
	id = SyriaFocus.46
	title = SyriaFocus.46.t
	desc = SyriaFocus.46.d
	picture = GFX_damascus_spring

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = SyriaFocus.46.a
		log = "[GetDateText]: [This.GetName]: SyriaFocus.46.a executed"
		ai_chance = {
			base = 20
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				OR = {
					has_global_flag = SYR_HISTORICAL_FOCUS_PATH
					has_global_flag = SYR_IRON_LADY_FOCUS_PATH
					has_global_flag = SYR_SYRIAN_SAVAGE_FOCUS_PATH
				}
			}
		}
		custom_effect_tooltip = TT_TEMPORARY_LEADER
		hidden_effect = {
			set_temp_variable = { election_year = global.year }
			add_to_temp_variable = { election_year = -3 }
			set_country_flag = Syrian_rebels
			meta_effect = {
				text = {
					set_politics = {
						ruling_party = neutrality
						elections_allowed = yes
						last_election = "[YEAR].[MONTH].1"
						election_frequency = 48
					}
				}
				YEAR = global.year
				MONTH = global.month
			}
		}
		add_popularity = { ideology = neutrality popularity = 0.2 }
		add_popularity = { ideology = democratic popularity = 0.2 }
		add_popularity = { ideology = nationalist popularity = 0.2 }
		custom_effect_tooltip = TT_SYR_EARLY_CIVIL_WAR_CHANCE
		set_temp_variable = { total_added_popularity = 0 }
		add_to_temp_variable = { total_added_popularity = added_bashar_popularity }
		add_to_temp_variable = { total_added_popularity = added_bushra_popularity }
		add_to_temp_variable = { total_added_popularity = added_maher_popularity }
		divide_temp_variable = { total_added_popularity = 3 }
		set_temp_variable = { civil_war_chance = total_added_popularity }
		multiply_temp_variable = { civil_war_chance = 2 }
		clamp_temp_variable = {
			var = civil_war_chance
			min = 0
			max = 100
		}
		set_temp_variable = { remaining_chance = 100 }
		subtract_from_temp_variable = { remaining_chance = civil_war_chance }
		clamp_temp_variable = {
			var = remaining_chance
			min = 0
			max = 100
		}
		random_list = {
			50 = {
				modifier = {
					factor = remaining_chance
				}
			}
			50 = {
				modifier = {
					factor = civil_war_chance
				}
				start_civil_war = {
					ideology = communism
					size = 0.3
				}
				set_global_flag = SYR_civil_war_started_early
				if = {
					limit = {
						LEB = { is_subject_of = ROOT }
					}
					set_autonomy = {
						target = LEB
						autonomy_state = autonomy_free
					}
				}
			}
		}
		hidden_effect = { news_event = { id = SyriaFocusNews.12 hours = 6 } }
	}
	option = {
		name = SyriaFocus.46.b
		log = "[GetDateText]: [This.GetName]: SyriaFocus.46.b executed"
		ai_chance = {
			base = 80
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				has_global_flag = SYR_NEW_ASSAD_FOCUS_PATH
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = Rifaat_no_international_support }
			random_list = {
				90 = { }
				10 = {
					if = {
						limit = { NOT = { has_government = neutrality } }
						start_civil_war = {
							ideology = neutrality
							size = 0.3
						}
					}
					if = {
						limit = { has_government = neutrality }
						start_civil_war = {
							ideology = democratic
							size = 0.3
						}
					}
					hidden_effect = {
						random_country = {
							limit = {
								original_tag = SYR
								NOT = { tag = SYR }
							}
							set_country_flag = Syrian_rebels
						}
					}
					set_global_flag = SYR_civil_war_started_early
					if = {
						limit = {
							LEB = { is_subject_of = ROOT }
						}
						set_autonomy = {
							target = LEB
							autonomy_state = autonomy_free
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = Rifaat_international_support }
			random_list = {
				80 = { }
				20 = {
					if = {
						limit = { NOT = { has_government = neutrality } }
						start_civil_war = {
							ideology = neutrality
							size = 0.5
						}
					}
					if = {
						limit = { has_government = neutrality }
						start_civil_war = {
							ideology = democratic
							size = 0.5
						}
					}
					hidden_effect = {
						random_country = {
							limit = {
								original_tag = SYR
								NOT = { tag = SYR }
							}
							set_country_flag = Syrian_rebels
						}
					}
					set_global_flag = SYR_civil_war_started_early
					every_country = {
						limit = {
							original_tag = SYR
							NOT = { has_idea = divided_syria }
						}
						add_ideas = divided_syria
					}
					if = {
						limit = {
							LEB = { is_subject_of = ROOT }
						}
						set_autonomy = {
							target = LEB
							autonomy_state = autonomy_free
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
		hidden_effect = { news_event = { id = SyriaFocusNews.13 hours = 6 } }
	}

}

#Succesful assassination of Rafic Hariri
country_event = {
	id = SyriaFocus.47
	title = SyriaFocus.47.t
	desc = SyriaFocus.47.d
	picture = GFX_rafic_hariri_assassination

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = SyriaFocus.47.a
		log = "[GetDateText]: [This.GetName]: SyriaFocus.47.a executed"
		ai_chance = { base = 100 }
		add_stability = -0.10
		add_opinion_modifier = {
			target = SYR
			modifier = barbarian_practices
		}
		hidden_effect = { news_event = { id = SyriaFocusNews.14 hours = 6 } }
		hidden_effect = { SYR = { country_event = { id = SyriaFocus.49 days = 14 } } }
	}

}

#Cedar Revolution
country_event = {
	id = SyriaFocus.49
	title = SyriaFocus.49.t
	desc = SyriaFocus.49.d
	picture = GFX_Syrian_Occupation_Lebanon

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = SyriaFocus.49.a
		log = "[GetDateText]: [This.GetName]: SyriaFocus.49.a executed"
		ai_chance = {
			base = 50
		}
		add_timed_idea = { idea = international_sanctions days = 730 }
		revoke_sanctions_non_exist_country = yes
	}

	option = {
		name = SyriaFocus.49.b
		log = "[GetDateText]: [This.GetName]: SyriaFocus.49.b executed"
		ai_chance = {
			base = 50
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				has_global_flag = SYR_SYRIAN_SAVAGE_FOCUS_PATH
			}
		}
		set_autonomy = {
			target = LEB
			autonomy_state = autonomy_free
		}
		LEB = {
			if = {
				limit = { has_government = democratic }
				add_popularity = { ideology = democratic popularity = -0.10 }
			}
			if = {
				limit = { has_government = communism }
				add_popularity = { ideology = communism popularity = -0.10 }
			}
			if = {
				limit = { has_government = neutrality }
				add_popularity = { ideology = neutrality popularity = -0.10 }
			}
			if = {
				limit = { has_government = nationalist }
				add_popularity = { ideology = nationalist popularity = -0.10 }
			}
			if = {
				limit = { has_government = fascism }
				add_popularity = { ideology = fascism popularity = -0.10 }
			}
			set_temp_variable = { year_change = 5 }
			set_election_year_60_months = yes
		}
		hidden_effect = { news_event = { id = SyriaFocusNews.16 hours = 6 } }
	}

}

#SSNP Coup in Jordan
country_event = {
	id = SyriaFocus.56
	title = SyriaFocus.56.t
	desc = SyriaFocus.56.d
	picture = GFX_SYR_ssnp

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		211 = { add_core_of = ROOT }
		210 = { add_core_of = ROOT }
		212 = { add_core_of = ROOT }
	}

	option = {
		name = SyriaFocus.56.a
		log = "[GetDateText]: [This.GetName]: SyriaFocus.56.a executed"
		ai_chance = { base = 80 }
		annex_country = {
			target = FROM
			transfer_troops = yes
		}
		add_named_threat = {
			threat = 5
			name = SYR.Jordan_coup
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = SyriaFocus.56.b		#Can resist as player
		log = "[GetDateText]: [This.GetName]: SyriaFocus.56.b executed"
		ai_chance = { base = 20 }
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_global_flag = GAME_RULE_allow_colored_puppets
			}
			set_autonomy = {
				target = FROM
				autonomy_state = autonomy_autonomous_state_colored
				end_wars = yes
				end_civil_wars = yes
			}
		}
		else = {
			set_autonomy = {
				target = FROM
				autonomy_state = autonomy_autonomous_state
				end_wars = yes
				end_civil_wars = yes
			}
		}
	}

}

#Join Syria in a war against Israel
country_event = {
	id = SyriaFocus.62
	title = SyriaFocus.62.t
	desc = SyriaFocus.62.d
	picture = GFX_palestinians_in_syria

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = SyriaFocus.62.a
		log = "[GetDateText]: [This.GetName]: SyriaFocus.62.a executed"
		ai_chance = { base = 90 }
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_global_flag = GAME_RULE_allow_colored_puppets
			}
			set_autonomy = {
				target = PREV
				autonomy_state = autonomy_puppet_state_colored
				end_wars = yes
				end_civil_wars = yes
			}
		}
		else = {
			set_autonomy = {
				target = PREV

#Syria willing to make concessions to Kurdistan - Kurdistan agrees
country_event = {
	id = SyriaFocus.67
	title = SyriaFocus.67.t
	desc = SyriaFocus.67.d
	picture = GFX_kurdistan

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = SyriaFocus.67.a
		log = "[GetDateText]: [This.GetName]: SyriaFocus.67.a executed"
		ai_chance = { base = 100 }
		if = {
			limit = { ROJ = { NOT = { is_subject_of = ROOT } } }
			every_owned_state = {
				limit = { is_core_of = KUR }
				KUR = { transfer_state = PREV }
			}
			every_owned_state = {
				limit = { is_core_of = KUR }
				add_core_of = ROOT
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { ROJ = { is_subject_of = ROOT } }
			KUR = { annex_country = { target = ROJ transfer_troops = yes } }
			every_owned_state = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						is_core_of = KUR
						is_core_of = ROJ
					}
				}
				KUR = { transfer_state = PREV }
			}
			every_state = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						is_core_of = KUR
						is_core_of = ROJ
					}
				}
				add_core_of = ROOT
			}
		}
		puppet = KUR
	}
}

# The Druze State Breaks Away
country_event = {
	id = SyriaFocus.97
	title = SyriaFocus.97.t
	desc = SyriaFocus.97.d
	picture = GFX_elections
	is_triggered_only = yes
	trigger = {
		original_tag = SYR
	}

	immediate = {
		set_country_flag = SYR_druze_has_spawned
	}

	# Alright
	option = {
		name = SyriaFocus.97.a #alright
		log = "[GetDateText]: [This.GetName]: SyriaFocus.97.a executed"
		ai_chance = { base = 50 }
		hidden_effect = {
			if = {
				limit = { DRU = { exists = no } }
				release = DRU
				DRU = { load_oob = DRU_2017 }
			}
			if = {
				limit = { DRU = { is_subject_of = ROOT } }
				set_autonomy = {
					target = DRU
					autonomy_state = autonomy_free
				}
			}
			#Set a random ideology
			DRU = {
				random_list = {
					10 = {
						set_politics = { ruling_party = democratic elections_allowed = yes }
						add_popularity = { ideology = democratic popularity = 0.6 }
					}
					20 = {
						set_politics = { ruling_party = communism elections_allowed = yes }
						add_popularity = { ideology = communism popularity = 0.6 }
					}
					40 = {
						set_politics = { ruling_party = neutrality elections_allowed = yes }
						add_popularity = { ideology = neutrality popularity = 0.6 }
					}
					10 = {
						set_politics = { ruling_party = nationalist elections_allowed = no }
						add_popularity = { ideology = nationalist popularity = 0.6 }
					}
				}
				recalculate_party = yes
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = SyriaFocus.97.b #fight them
		log = "[GetDateText]: [This.GetName]: SyriaFocus.97.a executed"
		ai_chance = { base = 50 }
		hidden_effect = {
			if = {
				limit = { DRU = { exists = no } }
				release = DRU
				DRU = { load_oob = DRU_2017 }
			}
			if = {
				limit = { DRU = { is_subject_of = ROOT } }
				set_autonomy = {
					target = DRU
					autonomy_state = autonomy_free
				}
			}
			#Set a random ideology
			DRU = {
				random_list = {
					10 = {
						set_politics = { ruling_party = democratic elections_allowed = yes }
						add_popularity = { ideology = democratic popularity = 0.6 }
					}
					20 = {
						set_politics = { ruling_party = communism elections_allowed = yes }
						add_popularity = { ideology = communism popularity = 0.6 }
					}
					40 = {
						set_politics = { ruling_party = neutrality elections_allowed = yes }
						add_popularity = { ideology = neutrality popularity = 0.6 }
					}
					10 = {
						set_politics = { ruling_party = nationalist elections_allowed = no }
						add_popularity = { ideology = nationalist popularity = 0.6 }
					}
				}
			}
		}
		declare_war_on = {
			target = DRU
			type = annex_everything
		}
	}
}

# Alawite Officers Break Away
country_event = {
	id = SyriaFocus.98
	title = SyriaFocus.98.t
	desc = SyriaFocus.98.d
	picture = GFX_alawite_officers_event
	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = SyriaFocus.98.a
		log = "[GetDateText]: [This.GetName]: SyriaFocus.98.a executed"
		if = {
			limit = { has_idea = alawite_high_command }
			remove_ideas = alawite_high_command
			remove_state_core = 189
			add_ideas = military_power_vacuum
			if = {
				limit = { has_idea = intelligence_community }
				set_temp_variable = { temp_opinion = -15 }
				change_intelligence_community_opinion = yes
			}
			if = {
				limit = { has_idea = iranian_quds_force }
				set_temp_variable = { temp_opinion = -10 }
				change_iranian_quds_force_opinion = yes
			}
			if = {
				limit = { has_idea = oligarchs }
				set_temp_variable = { temp_opinion = -5 }
				change_oligarchs_opinion = yes
			}
			if = {
				limit = { ALA = { exists = no } }
				release = ALA
				hidden_effect = {
					ALA = { load_oob = ALA_2017 }
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { ALA = { is_subject_of = ROOT } }
				set_autonomy = {
					target = ALA
					autonomy_state = autonomy_free
				}
			}
			ALA = {
				clear_array = ruling_party
				clear_array = gov_coalition_array
				add_to_array = { ruling_party = 22 }
				set_politics = { ruling_party = nationalist elections_allowed = no }
				add_popularity = { ideology = nationalist popularity = 0.6 }
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_idea = alawite_high_command_strengthened }
			remove_ideas = alawite_high_command_strengthened
			add_timed_idea = { idea = military_power_vacuum days = 140 }
			if = {
				limit = { has_idea = intelligence_community }
				set_temp_variable = { temp_opinion = -15 }
				change_intelligence_community_opinion = yes
			}
			if = {
				limit = { has_idea = iranian_quds_force }
				set_temp_variable = { temp_opinion = -10 }
				change_iranian_quds_force_opinion = yes
			}
			if = {
				limit = { has_idea = oligarchs }
				set_temp_variable = { temp_opinion = -5 }
				change_oligarchs_opinion = yes
			}
			start_civil_war = {
				ideology = nationalist
				size = 0.5
			}
			every_country = {
				limit = {
					original_tag = SYR
					NOT = { has_idea = alawite_high_command_strengthened }
				}
				remove_state_core = 189
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					LEB = { is_subject_of = ROOT }
				}
				set_autonomy = {
					target = LEB
					autonomy_state = autonomy_free
				}
			}
		}
		ai_chance = { base = 100 }
	}
}

#Start negotiations
country_event = {

	id = SyriaDecisions.0
	title = SyriaDecisions.0.t
	desc = SyriaDecisions.0.desc
	picture = GFX_Golan

	is_triggered_only = yes

	fire_only_once = yes

	option = {
		name = SyriaDecisions.0.a
		log = "[GetDateText]: [This.GetName]: SyriaDecisions.0.a executed"		#Agree
		ai_chance = {
			base = 60
			modifier = {
				factor = 2			#More helpful if it's the Druze asking
				FROM = {
					original_tag = DRU
				}
			}
		}
		FROM = { country_event = { id = SyriaDecisions.1 days = 3 } }
		FROM = {
			set_temp_variable = { percent_change = 2 }
			set_temp_variable = { tag_index = ROOT.id }
			set_temp_variable = { influence_target = THIS.id }
			change_influence_percentage = yes
		}
		if = {
			limit = { FROM = { original_tag = DRU } }
			add_ai_strategy = {
				type = support
				id = "DRU"
				value = 200
			}
			add_ai_strategy = {
				type = send_volunteers_desire
				id = "DRU"
				value = 200
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { FROM = { original_tag = FSA } }
			add_ai_strategy = {
				type = support
				id = "FSA"
				value = 200
			}
			add_ai_strategy = {
				type = send_volunteers_desire
				id = "FSA"
				value = 200
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = SyriaDecisions.0.b
		log = "[GetDateText]: [This.GetName]: SyriaDecisions.0.b executed"		#No
		ai_chance = {
			base = 40
			modifier = {
				factor = 0.5
				FROM = {
					original_tag = DRU
				}
			}
		}
		FROM = { country_event = { id = SyriaDecisions.2 days = 3 } }
	}
}

country_event = {

	id = SyriaDecisions.6

	title = SyriaDecisions.6.t
	desc = SyriaDecisions.6.desc
	picture = GFX_SYR_rojava

	is_triggered_only = yes

	fire_only_once = yes

	option = {
		name = SyriaDecisions.6.a
		log = "[GetDateText]: [This.GetName]: SyriaDecisions.6.a executed"		#Join the war
		ai_chance = {
			base = 80
			modifier = {
				factor = 0.125
				has_war = yes
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0.8
				FROM = { original_tag = SYR }
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				FROM = {
					original_tag = FSA
					has_war_with = SYR
				}
			}
			add_to_war = {
				targeted_alliance = FSA
				enemy = SYR
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				FROM = {
					original_tag = FSA
					has_war_with = NUS
				}
			}
			add_to_war = {
				targeted_alliance = FSA
				enemy = NUS
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				FROM = {
					original_tag = SYR
					has_war_with = FSA
				}
			}
			add_to_war = {
				targeted_alliance = SYR
				enemy = FSA
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				FROM = {
					original_tag = SYR
					has_war_with = NUS
				}
			}
			add_to_war = {
				targeted_alliance = SYR
				enemy = NUS
			}
		}
		FROM = { country_event = { id = SyriaDecisions.7 days = 3 } }
	}
	option = {
		name = SyriaDecisions.6.b
		log = "[GetDateText]: [This.GetName]: SyriaDecisions.6.b executed"		#No
		ai_chance = {
			base = 20
			modifier = {
				factor = 4.5
				has_war = yes
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 1.25
				FROM = { original_tag = SYR }
			}
		}
		FROM = { country_event = { id = SyriaDecisions.8 days = 3 } }
	}
}

		log = "[GetDateText]: [This.GetName]: SyriaDecisions.9.a executed"
		ai_chance = { base = 80 }
		random_country = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { original_tag = HEZ }
				has_country_flag = HEZ_Infiltrated_by_SYR
			}
			country_event = SyriaDecisions.10
			clr_country_flag = HEZ_Infiltrated_by_SYR
		}
		start_border_war = {
			change_state_after_war = no
			attacker = {
				state = 553
				num_provinces = 1
				on_win = SyriaDecisions.16
				on_lose = SyriaDecisions.17
				on_cancel = SyriaDecisions.17
			}
			defender = {
				state = 184
				num_provinces = 1
				on_win = SyriaDecisions.16
				on_lose = SyriaDecisions.17
				on_cancel = SyriaDecisions.17
			}
		}
		clr_country_flag = HEZ_Infiltrated_by_SYR
		hidden_effect = { news_event = { id = SyriaDecisionsNews.3 hours = 6 } }
	}
	option = {
		name = SyriaDecisions.9.b		#Return them and request compensation
		log = "[GetDateText]: [This.GetName]: SyriaDecisions.9.b executed"
		ai_chance = { base = 20 }
		random_country = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { original_tag = HEZ }
				has_country_flag = HEZ_Infiltrated_by_SYR
			}
			country_event = SyriaDecisions.11
			clr_country_flag = HEZ_Infiltrated_by_SYR
		}
		clr_country_flag = HEZ_Infiltrated_by_SYR
	}

}

country_event = {

	id = SyriaDecisions.15

	title = SyriaDecisions.15.t
	desc = SyriaDecisions.15.desc
	picture = GFX_united_arab_republic

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = SyriaDecisions.15.a		#
		log = "[GetDateText]: [This.GetName]: SyriaDecisions.15.a executed"
		ai_chance = { base = 100 }
		every_country = {
			limit = { is_subject_of = FROM }
			every_owned_state = {
				limit = { is_core_of = PREV }
				add_core_of = ROOT
			}
			ROOT = {
				annex_country = {
					target = PREV
					transfer_troops = yes
				}
			}
		}
		FROM = {
			every_owned_state = {
				limit = { is_core_of = PREV }
				add_core_of = ROOT
			}
		}
		annex_country = {
			target = FROM
			transfer_troops = yes
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = SyriaDecisions.15.b		#
		log = "[GetDateText]: [This.GetName]: SyriaDecisions.15.b executed"
		ai_chance = { base = 0 }
	}

}

#Canadian Investment
country_event = {
	id = syrian_economy.1
	title = syrian_economy.1.t
	desc = syrian_economy.1.d
	picture = GFX_generic_oil_field
	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = syrian_economy.1.a
		log = "[GetDateText]: [This.GetName]: syrian_economy.1.a executed"
		add_tech_bonus = {
			name = CAT_excavation_tech
			bonus = 0.25
			uses = 1
			category = CAT_excavation_tech
		}
		set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = -3.75 }
		modify_treasury_effect = yes
		set_temp_variable = { int_investment_change = 3.75 }
		modify_international_investment_effect = yes
		set_temp_variable = { percent_change = 1.5 }
		set_temp_variable = { tag_index = ROOT }
		set_temp_variable = { influence_target = SYR }
		change_influence_percentage = yes
		set_temp_variable = { modify_capitalization =  0.005 }
        modify_capitalization_support = yes  
		add_opinion_modifier = {
			target = SYR
			modifier = hungary_diplomatic_partnership
		}
		reverse_add_opinion_modifier = {
			target = CAN
			modifier = hungary_diplomatic_partnership
		}
		SYR = {
			country_event = syrian_economy.2
			188 = {
				add_resource = {
					type = oil
					amount = 4
				}
			}
		}   
		ai_chance = {
			base = 50
			modifier = {
				add = 50
				has_opinion = {
					target = SYR
					value > 10
				}
			}
			modifier = {
				add = -40
				has_opinion = {
					target = SYR
					value < 10
				}
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = syrian_economy.1.b
		log = "[GetDateText]: [This.GetName]: syrian_economy.1.b executed"
		SYR = {
			country_event = syrian_economy.3
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 50
			modifier = {
				add = -40
				has_opinion = {
					target = SYR
					value > 10
				}
			}
			modifier = {
				add = 50
				has_opinion = {
					target = SYR
					value < 10
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

		set_temp_variable = { tag_index = ROOT }
		set_temp_variable = { influence_target = SYR }
		change_influence_percentage = yes
		SYR = {
			country_event = syrian_economy.10
			set_temp_variable = { debt_change = -10.00 }
			modify_debt_effect = yes	
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 50
			modifier = {
				add = 50
				has_opinion = {
					target = SYR
					value > 10
				}
			}
			modifier = {
				add = -40
				has_opinion = {
					target = SYR
					value < 10
				}
			}
		}
	}

#Nuclear Invest
country_event = {
	id = syrian_economy.24
	title = syrian_economy.24.t
	desc = syrian_economy.24.d
	picture = GFX_nuclear_power_plant

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = syrian_economy.24.a
		log = "[GetDateText]: [This.GetName]:syrian_economy.24.a executed"
		if = {
			limit = { original_tag = NKO }
			SYR = {
				set_temp_variable = { temp_change = 1 }
				build_enrichment_facilities_effect = yes
			}
			set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = 25.00 }
			modify_treasury_effect = yes
		}
		if = {
			limit = { original_tag = ISR }
			SYR = {
				set_country_flag = SYR_ISR_nuclear_program
				add_tech_bonus = {
					name = SYR_boost_nuclear_research
					bonus = 1
					uses = 2
					category = CAT_nuclear_reactors
					category = CAT_aa
					category = CAT_l_drone
					category = CAT_computing_tech
     			}
				set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = -10.00 }
				modify_treasury_effect = yes
			}
			set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = 10.00 }
			modify_treasury_effect = yes
		}
		if = {
			limit = { original_tag = PAK }
				if = {
				limit = {
					has_dlc = "Gotterdammerung"
				}
				add_equipment_to_stockpile = {
					type = sam_missile_equipment_3
					producer = PAK
					amount = -100
				}
			}
			else = {
				add_equipment_to_stockpile = {
					type = ballistic_missile_equipment
					amount = -100
				}
			}
			set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = 10.00 }
            modify_treasury_effect = yes
			SYR = {
				add_tech_bonus = {
					name = SYR_boost_nuclear_research
					bonus = 0.5
					uses = 1
					category = CAT_nuclear_reactors
					category = CAT_nuclear_weapons
					category = CAT_missile
     			}
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_dlc = "Gotterdammerung"
					}
					add_equipment_to_stockpile = {
						type = sam_missile_equipment_3
						producer = PAK
						amount = 100
					}
				}
				else = {
					add_equipment_to_stockpile = {
						type = ballistic_missile_equipment
						amount = 100
					}
				}
				set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = -10.00 }
				modify_treasury_effect = yes
			}
		}
		SYR = {
			country_event = syrian_economy.25
		}
		add_opinion_modifier = {
			target = ROOT
			modifier = declaration_of_friendship
		}
		reverse_add_opinion_modifier = {
			target = SYR
			modifier = declaration_of_friendship
		}
		set_temp_variable = { percent_change = 3.0 }
		set_temp_variable = { tag_index = ROOT }
		set_temp_variable = { influence_target = SYR }
		change_influence_percentage = yes
		ai_chance = {
			base = 1
			modifier = {
				THIS = { has_opinion = { target = SYR value > 25 } }
				add = 10
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = syrian_economy.24.b
		log = "[GetDateText]: [This.GetName]: syrian_economy.24.b executed"
		SYR = {
			country_event = syrian_economy.26
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 1
		}
	}
}

						type = offices
						level = 2
						instant_build = yes
					}
				}
			}
		}

			base = 50
			modifier = {
				add = 50

					target = SYR
					value > 20
				}
			}
			modifier = {
				add = -40

					target = SYR
					value < 10
				}
			}

#Military Coop with Armenia
country_event = {
	id = syrian_diplomacy.29
	title = syrian_diplomacy.29.t
	desc = syrian_diplomacy.29.d
	picture = GFX_armenian_army_insyria
	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = syrian_diplomacy.29.a
		log = "[GetDateText]: [This.GetName]: syrian_diplomacy.29.a executed"
		set_temp_variable = { percent_change = 2.0 }
		set_temp_variable = { tag_index = ARM }
		set_temp_variable = { influence_target = SYR }
		change_influence_percentage = yes
		set_temp_variable = { percent_change = 2.0 }
		set_temp_variable = { tag_index = SYR }
		set_temp_variable = { influence_target = ARM }
		change_influence_percentage = yes
		give_military_access = SYR
		add_opinion_modifier = {
			target = SYR
			modifier = poland_military_partnership
		}
		reverse_add_opinion_modifier = {
			target = SYR
			modifier = poland_military_partnership
		} 
		SYR = {
			country_event = syrian_diplomacy.30
			give_military_access = ARM
			swap_ideas = {
				remove_idea = SYR_army_sso2
				add_idea = SYR_army_sso3
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 50
			modifier = {
				add = 50
				has_opinion = {
					target = SYR
					value > 10
				}
			}
			modifier = {
				add = -40
				has_opinion = {
					target = SYR
					value < 10
				}
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = syrian_diplomacy.29.b
		log = "[GetDateText]: [This.GetName]: syrian_diplomacy.29.b executed"
		SYR = {
			country_event = syrian_diplomacy.31
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 50
			modifier = {
				add = -40
				has_opinion = {
					target = SYR
					value > 10
				}
			}
			modifier = {
				add = 50
				has_opinion = {
					target = SYR
					value < 10
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

#Little Hagia Sophia Project
country_event = {
	id = syrian_diplomacy.32
	title = syrian_diplomacy.32.t
	desc = syrian_diplomacy.32.d
	picture = GFX_little_hagia_syr
	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = syrian_diplomacy.32.a
		log = "[GetDateText]: [This.GetName]: syrian_diplomacy.32.a executed"
		add_stability = 0.05
		add_political_power = 150
		add_opinion_modifier = {
			modifier = SYR_little_hagia_sophia_project
			target = ARM
		}
		reverse_add_opinion_modifier = {
			modifier = SYR_little_hagia_sophia_project
			target = ARM
		}
		add_opinion_modifier = {
			modifier = SYR_little_hagia_sophia_project
			target = SYR
		}
		reverse_add_opinion_modifier = {
			modifier = SYR_little_hagia_sophia_project
			target = SYR
		}
		set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = -1.5 }
		modify_treasury_effect = yes
		set_temp_variable = { temp_opinion = 5 }
		change_the_clergy_opinion = yes
		add_opinion_modifier = { target = TUR modifier = recent_actions_negative }
		reverse_add_opinion_modifier = { target = TUR modifier = recent_actions_negative }
		SYR = {
			country_event = syrian_diplomacy.33
			add_stability = 0.03
			add_political_power = 150
			add_opinion_modifier = {
				modifier = SYR_little_hagia_sophia_project
				target = ARM
			}
			reverse_add_opinion_modifier = {
				modifier = SYR_little_hagia_sophia_project
				target = ARM
			}
			add_opinion_modifier = {
				modifier = SYR_little_hagia_sophia_project
				target = SOV
			}
			reverse_add_opinion_modifier = {
				modifier = SYR_little_hagia_sophia_project
				target = SOV
			}
			set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = -1.5 }
			modify_treasury_effect = yes
			set_temp_variable = { temp_opinion = -2 }
			change_the_ulema_opinion = yes
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 50
			modifier = {
				add = 50
				has_opinion = {
					target = SYR
					value > 10
				}
			}
			modifier = {
				add = -40
				has_opinion = {
					target = SYR
					value < 10
				}
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = syrian_diplomacy.32.b
		log = "[GetDateText]: [This.GetName]: syrian_diplomacy.32.b executed"
		SYR = {
			country_event = syrian_economy.3
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 50
			modifier = {
				add = -40
				has_opinion = {
					target = SYR
					value > 10
				}
			}
			modifier = {
				add = 50
				has_opinion = {
					target = SYR
					value < 10
				}
			}
		}
	}

country_event = {
	id = syrian_diplomacy.34
	title = syrian_diplomacy.34.t
	desc = syrian_diplomacy.34.d
	picture = GFX_hezbollah_influence
	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = syrian_diplomacy.34.a
		log = "[GetDateText]: [This.GetName]: syrian_diplomacy.34.a executed"
		set_temp_variable = { percent_change = 2.0 }
		set_temp_variable = { tag_index = PER }
		set_temp_variable = { influence_target = SYR }
		change_influence_percentage = yes
		set_temp_variable = { percent_change = 2.0 }
		set_temp_variable = { tag_index = SYR }
		set_temp_variable = { influence_target = PER }
		change_influence_percentage = yes
		add_manpower = -6000
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = {
			type = Inf_equipment
			amount = -10000
			producer = PER
		}
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = {
			type = cnc_equipment
			amount = -1500
			producer = PER
		}
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = {
			type = util_vehicle_equipment
			amount = -250
			producer = PER
		}
		SYR = {
			country_event = syrian_diplomacy.35
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = {
				type = Inf_equipment
				amount = 10000
				producer = PER
			}
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = {
				type = cnc_equipment
				amount = 1500
				producer = PER
			}
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = {
				type = util_vehicle_equipment
				amount = 250
				producer = PER
			}
			division_template = {
				name = "Niru-ye Qods-e Sepah-e Pasdaran"
				is_locked = yes
				regiments = {
					Special_Forces = { x = 0 y = 0 }
					Special_Forces = { x = 0 y = 1 }
					Special_Forces = { x = 0 y = 2 }
					Special_Forces = { x = 0 y = 3 }
					Special_Forces = { x = 1 y = 0 }
					Special_Forces = { x = 1 y = 1 }
					Special_Forces = { x = 1 y = 2 }
					Special_Forces = { x = 1 y = 3 }
				}
				priority = 2
			}
			random_owned_controlled_state = {
				create_unit = {
					division = "name = \"Niru-ye Qods-e Sepah-e Pasdaran\" division_template = \"Niru-ye Qods-e Sepah-e Pasdaran\" start_experience_factor = 1"
					owner = SYR
				}
			}
		}
		HEZ = {
			country_event = syrian_diplomacy.35
			add_ideas = SYR_HEZ_supply_idea	
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 50
			modifier = {
				add = 50
				has_opinion = {
					target = SYR
					value > 50
				}
			}
			modifier = {
				add = -40
				has_opinion = {
					target = SYR
					value < 30
				}
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = syrian_diplomacy.34.b
		log = "[GetDateText]: [This.GetName]: syrian_diplomacy.34.b executed"
		SYR = {
			country_event = syrian_diplomacy.36
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 50
			modifier = {
				add = -50
				has_opinion = {
					target = SYR
					value > 30
				}
			}
			modifier = {
				add = 50
				has_opinion = {
					target = SYR
					value < 20
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

country_event = {
	id = syrian_democrats.1
	title = syrian_democrats.1.t
	desc = syrian_democrats.1.d
	picture = GFX_syrian_opposition_event
	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = syrian_democrats.1.a
		log = "[GetDateText]: [This.GetName]: syrian_democrats.1.a executed"
		if = {
			limit = { SYR = { has_war = yes } }
			add_manpower = -10000
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = {
				type = Inf_equipment
				amount = -3200
			}
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = {
				type = command_control_equipment
				amount = -300
			}
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = {
				type = artillery_equipment
				amount = -72
			}
			SYR = {
				add_ideas = SYR_us_peacekeepers 
				division_template = {
					name = "American Peacekeepers"
					is_locked = yes
					regiments = {
						L_Inf_Bat = { x = 0 y = 0 }
						L_Inf_Bat = { x = 0 y = 1 }
						L_Inf_Bat = { x = 0 y = 2 }
						L_Inf_Bat = { x = 1 y = 0 }
						L_Inf_Bat = { x = 1 y = 1 }
						L_Inf_Bat = { x = 1 y = 2 }
						Arty_Bat = { x = 2 y = 0 }
						Arty_Bat = { x = 2 y = 1 }
					}
					support = {
						L_Recce_Comp = { x = 0 y = 0}
						L_Engi_Comp = { x = 0 y = 1 }
					}
					priority = 2
				}
				random_owned_controlled_state = {
					create_unit = {
						division = "name = \"1st Battalion of American Peacekeepers\" division_template = \"American Peacekeepers\" start_experience_factor = 0.5"
						owner = SYR
					}
				}
				random_owned_controlled_state = {
					create_unit = {
						division = "name = \"2nd Battalion of American Peacekeepers\" division_template = \"American Peacekeepers\" start_experience_factor = 0.5"
						owner = SYR
					}
				}
			}
		}
		else = {
			USA = {
				give_guarantee = SYR
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { percent_change = 2 }
		set_temp_variable = { tag_index = USA }
		set_temp_variable = { influence_target = SYR }
		change_influence_percentage = yes
		add_opinion_modifier = {
			target = SYR
			modifier = military_cooperation
		}
		reverse_add_opinion_modifier = {
			target = SYR
			modifier = military_cooperation
		}
		SYR = {
			country_event = syrian_democrats.2
			give_military_access = USA
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 10
			modifier = {
				add = -100
				has_opinion = {
					target = SYR
					value < 10
				}
			}
			modifier = {
				add = 100
				has_opinion = {
					target = SYR
					value > 70
				}
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = syrian_democrats.1.b
		log = "[GetDateText]: [This.GetName]: syrian_democrats.1.b executed"
		SYR = {
			country_event = syrian_democrats.3
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 5
			modifier = {
				add = 100
				has_opinion = {
					target = SYR
					value < 10
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

		}

			threat = -2
			name = SYR_al_qaeda_reject
		}

			add_idea = al_qaeda_network1
		}

			limit = { has_idea = NATO_member }

	desc = syria_baranists.9.d

	is_triggered_only = yes

		name = syria_baranists.9.a
		log = "[GetDateText]: [This.GetName]: syria_baranists.9.a executed"
		if = {
			limit = { FSA = { is_subject_of = TUR } }
			set_temp_variable = { percent_change = 20 }
			set_temp_variable = { tag_index = TUR }
			set_temp_variable = { influence_target = FROM }
			change_influence_percentage = yes
		}
		FROM = {
			annex_country = {
				target = FSA
				transfer_troops = yes
			}
			country_event = syria_baranists.10
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = syria_baranists.9.b
		log = "[GetDateText]: [This.GetName]: syria_baranists.9.b executed"
		FROM = { country_event = syria_baranists.11 }
	}
}

country_event = {
	id = SyriaFocus.200
	title = SyriaFocus.200.t
	desc = SyriaFocus.200.d
	picture = GFX_EventCountry_Template
	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = SyriaFocus.200.a
		log = "[GetDateText]: [This.GetName]: SyriaFocus.200.a executed"
		set_temp_variable = { party_index = 11 }
		ban_party_scripted_call = yes
		set_temp_variable = { party_index = 13 }
		ban_party_scripted_call = yes

		hidden_effect = {
			TUR = {
				add_opinion_modifier = {
					modifier = hate_us
					target = SYR
				}
				send_embargo = SYR
			}
	
			QAT = {
				add_opinion_modifier = {
					modifier = hate_us
					target = SYR
				}
				send_embargo = SYR
			}
	
			SAU = {
				add_opinion_modifier = {
					modifier = hate_us
					target = SYR
				}
				send_embargo = SYR
			}

			random_list = {
				40 = {
					set_temp_variable = { percent_change = 10 }
					change_domestic_influence_percentage = yes
				}
				30 = {
					set_temp_variable = { percent_change = 15 }
					change_domestic_influence_percentage = yes
				}
				20 = {
					set_temp_variable = { percent_change = 20 }
					change_domestic_influence_percentage = yes
				}
				10 = {
					set_temp_variable = { percent_change = 25 }
					change_domestic_influence_percentage = yes
				}
			}
		}

		custom_effect_tooltip = SyriaFocus.200.tt
	}
	option = {
		name = SyriaFocus.200.b
		log = "[GetDateText]: [This.GetName]: SyriaFocus.200.b executed"
		add_political_power = -100
	}
}

	}

			idea = SYR_glory_of_uruk_idea

		}

	id = syria_mejd.4
	title = syria_mejd.4.t
	desc = syria_mejd.4.d
	picture = GFX_syria_medj_madness
	is_triggered_only = yes
	trigger  = {
    	nationalist_monarchists_are_in_power = yes
  	}

	option = {
		name = syria_mejd.4.a
		log = "[GetDateText]: [This.GetName]: syria_mejd.4.a executed"
		if = {
			limit = { has_idea = SYR_mejd_insanity_idea }
			swap_ideas = {
				remove_idea = SYR_mejd_insanity_idea
				add_idea = SYR_mejd_insanity1_idea
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { has_idea = SYR_mejd_insanity1_idea }
			swap_ideas = {
				remove_idea = SYR_mejd_insanity1_idea
				add_idea = SYR_mejd_insanity2_idea
			}
		}
	}
}
#Words of a Mental Breakdown
country_event = {
	id = syria_mejd.5
	title = syria_mejd.5.t
	desc = syria_mejd.5.d
	picture = GFX_syria_medj_madness
	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = syria_mejd.5.a
		log = "[GetDateText]: [This.GetName]: syria_mejd.5.a executed"
		set_temp_variable = { party_index = 23 }
		set_temp_variable = { party_popularity_increase = 0.25 }
		set_temp_variable = { temp_outlook_increase = 0.25 }
		add_relative_party_popularity = yes
		add_ideas = SYR_new_ashur_idea
		hidden_effect = {
			set_temp_variable = { party_index = 23 }
			set_temp_variable = { party_popularity_increase = party_pop_array^23 }
			add_relative_party_popularity = yes
			clear_array = ruling_party
			clear_array = gov_coalition_array
			add_to_array = { ruling_party = 23 }
			add_to_array = { gov_coalition_array = 13 }
			add_to_array = { gov_coalition_array = 22 }
			update_government_coalition_strength = yes
			update_party_name = yes
			set_coalition_drift = yes
			set_ruling_leader = yes
			set_leader = yes
			set_politics = {
				ruling_party = nationalist
				elections_allowed = no
			}
			create_country_leader = {
				name = "Gilgamesh"
				picture = "mejd_al_gilgamesh.dds"
				ideology = Monarchist
				traits = {
					nationalist_Monarchist
					inexperienced_imperialist
					incapable
					king
					warmonger
					personal_psychiatrist
				}
			}
		}
		recalculate_party = yes
	}
}
